  <!-- ===================================================================== -->
  <!-- 5. LOGIKA APLIKASI JAVASCRIPT (CLIENT STATE, API, & UX ENGINE) -->
  <!-- ===================================================================== -->
  <script>
    /**
     * STATE MANAGEMENT CLIENT-SIDE
     * Dilengkapi In-Memory Caching untuk perpindahan menu 0-detik (instant)
     */
    const appState = {
      currentUser: null, // null (Publik) atau { username, role, namaLengkap, userId }
      activeMenu: 'beranda',
      izinSubTab: 'aktif',
      penangananSubTab: 'sakit',
      kebutuhanSubTab: 'usulan',
      settings: {
        NAMA_INSTANSI: 'SRT 1 Sidenreng Rappang Jenjang SRMA',
        LOGO_URL: 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png',
        ALAMAT: 'Jl. Poros Rappang, Kab. Sidenreng Rappang, Sulawesi Selatan',
        KONTAK: '0812-3456-7890 / srt1sidrap@sekolahrakyat.sch.id'
      },
      metrics: null,
      cache: {
        wali: null,
        siswa: null,
        absensi: null,
        izin: null,
        sakit: null,
        pelanggaran: null,
        kebutuhan: null,
        inventaris: null
      },
      html5QrCode: null,
      chartInstance: null
    };

    // Inisialisasi Saat Halaman Dimuat
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      // Set input tanggal hari ini secara default
      const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
      const absTgl = document.getElementById('absensiTanggalInput');
      if (absTgl) absTgl.value = today;

      // Cek sesi login tersimpan di localStorage
      const savedUser = localStorage.getItem('esowali_user');
      if (savedUser) {
        try {
          appState.currentUser = JSON.parse(savedUser);
        } catch (e) {
          appState.currentUser = null;
        }
      }
      updateAuthUI();

      // Ambil data awal dari backend
      fetchInitialData();
      initCard3DTilt();
    });

    // =====================================================================
    // KONFIGURASI API BACKEND (GOOGLE APPS SCRIPT WEB APP)
    // =====================================================================
    // URL Web App Google Apps Script Anda (akhiran /exec)
    const DEFAULT_GAS_API_URL = 'https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbzT4QEsXqxsN6ISIrgb_hY1ctcPIZPV_3_md3mH2hT93GYaRt-9repZW-5tekW2VaT/exec';

    function getGasApiUrl() {
      return localStorage.getItem('esowali_api_url') || DEFAULT_GAS_API_URL;
    }

    function setGasApiUrl(newUrl) {
      if (newUrl && newUrl.trim()) {
        localStorage.setItem('esowali_api_url', newUrl.trim());
      }
    }

    /**
     * Panggilan Backend Aman (Safe Preview Mode Friendly)
     * Panggilan Backend Universal (Mendukung Google Apps Script Asli & GitHub Pages via CORS fetch)
     */
    function callBackend(functionName, args, callback, errorCallback) {
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
        const isGasNative = typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run;
        const isExternalHost = location.protocol !== 'blob:' && (location.hostname !== 'script.google.com' && location.hostname !== 'script.googleusercontent.com');

        // 1. Jika dijalankan langsung di dalam lingkungan Google Apps Script Web App
        if (isGasNative && !isExternalHost) {
          let runner = google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function (response) {
              if (callback) callback(response);
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function (err) {
              showToast('Kesalahan server: ' + err.message, 'error');
              if (errorCallback) errorCallback(err);
            });

          if (args && Array.isArray(args)) {
            runner[functionName].apply(runner, args);
          } else if (args !== undefined) {
            runner[functionName](args);
            let safeArgs = args !== undefined ? (Array.isArray(args) ? args : [args]) : [];
            if (safeArgs.length > 0) {
              runner[functionName].apply(runner, safeArgs);
            } else {
              runner[functionName]();
            }
          } else {
            // Fallback Simulasi Lokal jika dibuka langsung di Browser / Canvas
            return;
          }

          // 2. Jika dihosting di GitHub Pages / Custom Domain / Localhost
          const apiUrl = getGasApiUrl();
          if (!apiUrl) {
            simulateBackendLocally(functionName, args, callback);
            return;
          }

          const payload = {
            action: functionName,
            args: args !== undefined ? (Array.isArray(args) ? args : [args]) : []
          };

          // Mengirimkan CORS Simple Request (text/plain) agar browser tidak mengirimkan preflight OPTIONS
          fetch(apiUrl, {
            method: 'POST',
            mode: 'cors',
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
          })
            .then(function (res) {
              if (!res.ok) {
                throw new Error('HTTP Status: ' + res.status);
              }
              return res.json();
            })
            .then(function (data) {
              if (callback) callback(data);
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
              console.error('Fetch API error [' + functionName + ']:', err);
              showToast('Gagal menghubungi API server: ' + (err.message || err), 'error');
              if (errorCallback) errorCallback(err);
            });
        }

        /**
         * Memuat Data Awal Pengaturan dan Metrik Dashboard
         */
        function fetchInitialData() {
          callBackend('apiGetInitialData', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              if (res.settings) {
                appState.settings = Object.assign(appState.settings, res.settings);
                syncSettingsToUI();
              }
              if (res.metrics) {
                appState.metrics = res.metrics;
                renderMetrics(res.metrics);
              }
              if (res.serverTime) {
                const timeEl = document.getElementById('dashboardServerTime');
                if (timeEl) timeEl.innerText = res.serverTime;
              }
            }
          });
          // Ambil data wali untuk pipeline dashboard & dropdown surat izin
          fetchWaliData();
        }

        /**
         * Memformat Tautan Gambar & Mengubah URL Google Drive ke Direct CDN
         * Menghilangkan kendala X-Frame-Options dan gambar rusak (broken image).
         */
        function formatImageUrl(url, defaultAvatar) {
          if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return defaultAvatar || 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png';
          const trimmed = url.trim();
          if (!trimmed) return defaultAvatar || 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png';

          // Jika berupa DataURI Base64, kembalikan langsung
          if (trimmed.indexOf('data:') === 0) return trimmed;

          // Konversi berbagai variasi link Google Drive ke CDN thumbnail langsung
          if (trimmed.indexOf('drive.google.com') !== -1 || trimmed.indexOf('drive.usercontent.google.com') !== -1) {
            let id = '';
            const matchD = trimmed.match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
            if (matchD && matchD[1]) {
              id = matchD[1];
            } else {
              const matchId = trimmed.match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
              if (matchId && matchId[1]) id = matchId[1];
            }
            if (id) {
              return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=' + id + '&sz=w1000';
            }
          }
          return trimmed;
        }

        /**
         * Kompresi Gambar Otomatis Sisi Klien Sebelum Diunggah
         * Mengurangi ukuran file foto kamera HP (5-15MB) menjadi ~150-250KB beresolusi tajam (maks 1000px).
         * 100% mencegah error batas payload Google Apps Script dan menjamin proses upload kilat.
         */
        function compressImageFile(file, maxWidth, maxHeight, quality, callback) {
          if (!file) {
            callback('');
            return;
          }
          if (!file.type || !file.type.match(/image.*/)) {
            const r = new FileReader();
            r.onload = function (e) { callback(e.target.result); };
            r.onerror = function () { callback(''); };
            r.readAsDataURL(file);
            return;
          }

          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = function (readerEvent) {
            const image = new Image();
            image.onload = function () {
              let width = image.width;
              let height = image.height;
              const maxW = maxWidth || 1000;
              const maxH = maxHeight || 1000;

              if (width > height) {
                if (width > maxW) {
                  height = Math.round((height * maxW) / width);
                  width = maxW;
                }
              } else {
                if (height > maxH) {
                  width = Math.round((width * maxH) / height);
                  height = maxH;
                }
              }

              const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
              canvas.width = width;
              canvas.height = height;
              const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
              ctx.drawImage(image, 0, 0, width, height);

              // Kompresi JPEG kualitas 0.78 (tajam dan ukuran ~150KB)
              const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality || 0.78);
              callback(dataUrl);
            };
            image.onerror = function () {
              callback(readerEvent.target.result);
            };
            image.src = readerEvent.target.result;
          };
          reader.onerror = function () {
            callback('');
          };
          reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }

        /**
         * Sinkronisasi Pengaturan & Logo ke Navbar dan Header
         */
        function syncSettingsToUI() {
          const logoEl = document.getElementById('navLogoImg');
          const instansiEl = document.getElementById('navInstansiName');
          const heroInstansi = document.getElementById('heroInstansiName');
          const idLogo = document.getElementById('idCardLogo');
          const kopLogo = document.getElementById('portoKopLogo');
          const suratLogo = document.getElementById('suratKopLogo');

          const logoUrl = formatImageUrl(appState.settings.LOGO_URL, 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png');
          const instansi = appState.settings.NAMA_INSTANSI || 'SRT 1 Sidenreng Rappang';

          if (logoEl) logoEl.src = logoUrl;
          if (instansiEl) instansiEl.innerText = instansi;
          if (heroInstansi) heroInstansi.innerText = instansi;
          if (idLogo) idLogo.src = logoUrl;
          if (kopLogo) kopLogo.src = logoUrl;
          if (suratLogo) suratLogo.src = logoUrl;
        }

        /**
         * Render Angka Kartu Metrik dan Widget Grafik Donat Chart.js
         */
        function renderMetrics(m) {
          if (!m) return;
          // Wali Asuh
          setEl('cardWaliAsuhTotal', m.waliAsuh.total);
          setEl('cardWaliAsuhL', m.waliAsuh.L);
          setEl('cardWaliAsuhP', m.waliAsuh.P);

          // Wali Asrama
          setEl('cardWaliAsramaTotal', m.waliAsrama.total);
          setEl('cardWaliAsramaL', m.waliAsrama.L);
          setEl('cardWaliAsramaP', m.waliAsrama.P);

          // Total Siswa
          setEl('cardTotalSiswa', m.totalSiswa.total);
          setEl('cardTotalSiswaL', m.totalSiswa.L);
          setEl('cardTotalSiswaP', m.totalSiswa.P);

          // Hadir di Asrama
          setEl('cardHadirTotal', m.hadirAsrama.total);
          setEl('cardHadirL', m.hadirAsrama.L);
          setEl('cardHadirP', m.hadirAsrama.P);

          // Izin Rutin
          setEl('cardIzinRutinTotal', m.izinRutin.total);
          setEl('cardIzinRutinL', m.izinRutin.L);
          setEl('cardIzinRutinP', m.izinRutin.P);

          // Izin Darurat
          setEl('cardIzinDaruratTotal', m.izinDarurat.total);
          setEl('cardIzinDaruratL', m.izinDarurat.L);
          setEl('cardIzinDaruratP', m.izinDarurat.P);

          // Izin Keluar
          setEl('cardIzinKeluarTotal', m.izinKeluar.total);
          setEl('cardIzinKeluarL', m.izinKeluar.L);
          setEl('cardIzinKeluarP', m.izinKeluar.P);

          // Sakit Asrama
          setEl('cardSakitTotal', m.sakitAsrama.total);
          setEl('cardSakitL', m.sakitAsrama.L);
          setEl('cardSakitP', m.sakitAsrama.P);

          // Jalankan Rendering Widget Chart.js Doughnut 2-Kolom
          renderDoughnutChart(m);
          initCard3DTilt();
        }

        /**
         * Inisialisasi & Pembaharuan Grafik Chart.js Doughnut 2-Kolom Bebas Error
         */
        function renderDoughnutChart(m) {
          if (!m) return;
          const canvas = document.getElementById('chartDoughnutSiswa');
          if (!canvas) return;

          const total = m.totalSiswa.total || 0;
          const hadir = m.hadirAsrama.total || 0;
          const rutin = m.izinRutin.total || 0;
          const darurat = m.izinDarurat.total || 0;
          const keluar = m.izinKeluar.total || 0;

          // Hitung Persentase Realtime
          const pctHadir = total > 0 ? Math.round((hadir / total) * 100) : 0;
          const pctRutin = total > 0 ? Math.round((rutin / total) * 100) : 0;
          const pctDarurat = total > 0 ? Math.round((darurat / total) * 100) : 0;
          const pctKeluar = total > 0 ? Math.round((keluar / total) * 100) : 0;

          // Update Center Teks
          setEl('chartCenterNumber', total);
          setEl('chartCenterHadirPct', pctHadir + '% Hadir');

          // Update 4 Kartu Legenda Interaktif
          setEl('legCountHadir', hadir);
          setEl('legPctHadir', pctHadir + '%');

          setEl('legCountRutin', rutin);
          setEl('legPctRutin', pctRutin + '%');

          setEl('legCountDarurat', darurat);
          setEl('legPctDarurat', pctDarurat + '%');

          setEl('legCountKeluar', keluar);
          setEl('legPctKeluar', pctKeluar + '%');

          // Safe Check: Tunggu Chart.js jika script CDN masih proses memuat
          if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') {
            setTimeout(function () { renderDoughnutChart(m); }, 350);
            return;
          }

          // Hancurkan instance lama jika ada agar tidak memory-leak atau error canvas in-use
          if (appState.chartInstance) {
            appState.chartInstance.destroy();
            appState.chartInstance = null;
          }

          const rawValues = [hadir, rutin, darurat, keluar];
          const isAllZero = rawValues.every(function (v) { return v === 0; });
          const chartValues = isAllZero ? [1, 0, 0, 0] : rawValues;
          const chartColors = isAllZero
            ? ['#E2E8F0', '#E2E8F0', '#E2E8F0', '#E2E8F0']
            : ['#10b981', '#38bdf8', '#a78bfa', '#f59e0b'];

          const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
          appState.chartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
            type: 'doughnut',
            data: {
              labels: ['Hadir di Asrama', 'Izin Pulang Rutin', 'Izin Pulang Darurat/Sakit', 'Izin Keluar Ringkas'],
              datasets: [{
                data: chartValues,
                backgroundColor: chartColors,
                borderColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderWidth: 2.5,
                hoverOffset: 6
              }]
            },
            options: {
              responsive: true,
              maintainAspectRatio: false,
              cutout: '72%',
              plugins: {
                legend: {
                  display: false // Menggunakan daftar 4 kartu legenda kustom di kolom kanan
                },
                tooltip: {
                  enabled: !isAllZero,
                  backgroundColor: '#0F2537',
                  titleFont: { family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', size: 12, weight: 'bold' },
                  bodyFont: { family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', size: 11 },
                  padding: 10,
                  cornerRadius: 8,
                  callbacks: {
                    label: function (context) {
                      const val = rawValues[context.dataIndex] || 0;
                      const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((val / total) * 100) : 0;
                      return ' ' + context.label + ': ' + val + ' Siswa (' + pct + '%)';
                    }
                  }
                }
              },
              animation: {
                animateScale: true,
                animateRotate: true
              }
            }
          });
        }

        function highlightChartSegment(idx) {
          if (appState.chartInstance && appState.chartInstance.setActiveElements) {
            appState.chartInstance.setActiveElements([{ datasetIndex: 0, index: idx }]);
            appState.chartInstance.update();
          }
        }

        function resetChartHighlight() {
          if (appState.chartInstance && appState.chartInstance.setActiveElements) {
            appState.chartInstance.setActiveElements([]);
            appState.chartInstance.update();
          }
        }

        function setEl(id, val) {
          const el = document.getElementById(id);
          if (el) el.innerText = val;
        }

        /**
         * Efek 3D Perspective Tilt & Diagonal Glare Shine Interaktif pada Kartu Dashboard
         */
        function initCard3DTilt() {
          const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.stat-card-3d');
          cards.forEach(function (card) {
            if (card.dataset.tiltInitialized) return;
            card.dataset.tiltInitialized = 'true';

            const glare = card.querySelector('.glare-overlay');

            card.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
              const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
              const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
              const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
              const centerX = rect.width / 2;
              const centerY = rect.height / 2;

              const rotateX = ((centerY - y) / centerY * 8.5).toFixed(2);
              const rotateY = ((x - centerX) / centerX * 8.5).toFixed(2);

              card.style.transform = 'perspective(1000px) rotateX(' + rotateX + 'deg) rotateY(' + rotateY + 'deg) translateY(-4px)';

              if (glare) {
                const pctX = (x / rect.width) * 100;
                const pctY = (y / rect.height) * 100;
                glare.style.opacity = '1';
                glare.style.background = 'radial-gradient(circle at ' + pctX + '% ' + pctY + '%, rgba(255,255,255,0.4) 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0) 70%)';
              }
            });

            card.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
              card.style.transform = 'perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg) translateY(0px)';
              if (glare) {
                glare.style.opacity = '0';
              }
            });
          });
        }

        /**
         * Render Mini Pipeline Wali Asuh & Wali Asrama 5 Kolom Horizontal di Dashboard
         */
        function renderWaliPipeline(list) {

            container.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-6 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">Belum ada data wali asuh & wali asrama terdaftar.</div>';
            return;
          }
          container.innerHTML = '';

          list.forEach(function (item) {
            const waNumber = (item.noWhatsApp || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
            const cleanWa = waNumber.startsWith('0') ? '62' + waNumber.substring(1) : waNumber;
            const waLink = cleanWa ? 'https://wa.me/' + cleanWa : '#';
            const defaultAvatar = item.jenisKelamin === 'Perempuan'
              ? 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/4140/4140047.png'
              : 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png';

              ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 via-indigo-400 to-purple-500'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-maroon via-red-500 to-amber-500';

            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'min-w-[260px] sm:min-w-[280px] flex-shrink-0 bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200/90 shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3 group relative overflow-hidden hover:border-slate-300';

              '</div>' +
              '<div class="flex-1 min-w-0 leading-tight space-y-1">' +
              '<span class="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[9px] font-black ' + badgeClass + ' uppercase tracking-wider">' + (item.jabatan || 'Wali Asuh') + '</span>' +
              '<h4 class="font-extrabold text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-900 truncate leading-snug group-hover:text-navy transition-colors" title="' + item.nama + '">' + item.nama + '</h4>' +
              '<div class="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-[10px] text-slate-600 font-mono"><svg class="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-slate-400 flex-shrink-0" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 2a1 1 0 00-1 1v1a1 1 0 002 0V3a1 1 0 00-1-1zM4 4h3a3 3 0 006 0h3a2 2 0 012 2v9a2 2 0 01-2 2H4a2 2 0 01-2-2V6a2 2 0 012-2zm2.5 7a1.5 1.5 0 100-3 1.5 1.5 0 000 3zm2.45 4a2.5 2.5 0 00-4.9 0h4.9zM12 9a1 1 0 100 2h3a1 1 0 100-2h-3zm-1 4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 110 2h-2a1 1 0 01-1-1z" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span>NIP: ' + (item.nip || '-') + '</span></div>' +
              '</div>' +

              '<span class="text-[11px] font-semibold truncate">' + (item.noWhatsApp || 'No WA -') + '</span>' +

              (cleanWa ?               '</div>';
            container.appendChild(card);
          });
        }

        /**
         * Navigasi Menu Horizontal (0-Detik Response via In-Memory Cache)
         * Dilengkapi Transisi Halus, Active Pill Highlight & Ikon Reaktif
         */
        function navigateMenu(menuId) {
          const authOnlyMenus = ['absensi', 'izin', 'penanganan', 'kebutuhan'];
          if (!appState.currentUser && authOnlyMenus.indexOf(menuId) !== -1) {
            showToast('Menu ini khusus untuk petugas terdaftar. Silakan login terlebih dahulu.', 'warning');
            openModalLogin();
            return;
          }

          appState.activeMenu = menuId;

          const views = ['beranda', 'dashboard', 'wali', 'siswa', 'absensi', 'izin', 'penanganan', 'kebutuhan'];

          views.forEach(function (v) {
            // Sembunyikan section view
            const sec = document.getElementById('view-' + v);
            if (sec) sec.classList.add('hidden');

            // Reset tombol navigasi desktop
            const navBtn = document.getElementById('nav-' + v);
            if (navBtn) {
              navBtn.className = 'nav-btn flex-shrink-0 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10';
              const svg = navBtn.querySelector('svg');
              if (svg) {
                svg.classList.remove('text-amber-300');
                svg.classList.add('text-slate-400');
              }
            }

            // Reset tombol navigasi mobile drawer
            const mobBtn = document.getElementById('mobnav-' + v);
            if (mobBtn) {
              mobBtn.className = 'w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-300 hover:text-white font-medium flex items-center space-x-3 hover:bg-white/10 transition-all';
              const mobSvg = mobBtn.querySelector('svg');
              if (mobSvg) {
                mobSvg.classList.remove('text-amber-300');
                mobSvg.classList.add('text-slate-400');
              }
            }
          });

          // Aktifkan section view terpilih
          const targetSec = document.getElementById('view-' + menuId);
          if (targetSec) targetSec.classList.remove('hidden');

          // Aktifkan tombol navigasi desktop
          const targetNav = document.getElementById('nav-' + menuId);
          if (targetNav) {
            targetNav.className = "nav-btn flex-shrink-0 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-white/15 text-white ring-1 ring-white/20 shadow-inner relative after:content-[''] after:absolute after:bottom-0 after:left-3 after:right-3 after:h-0.5 after:bg-amber-400 after:rounded-full";
            const svg = targetNav.querySelector('svg');
            if (svg) {
              svg.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
              svg.classList.add('text-amber-300');
            }
          }

          // Aktifkan tombol navigasi mobile drawer
          const targetMob = document.getElementById('mobnav-' + menuId);
          if (targetMob) {
            targetMob.className = 'w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-white font-bold flex items-center space-x-3 bg-white/15 ring-1 ring-white/20 shadow-sm transition-all';
            const mobSvg = targetMob.querySelector('svg');
            if (mobSvg) {
              mobSvg.classList.remove('text-slate-400');
              mobSvg.classList.add('text-amber-300');
            }
          }

          // Tutup menu mobile jika sedang terbuka
          const mobMenu = document.getElementById('mobileMenu');
          if (mobMenu) mobMenu.classList.add('hidden');

          // Ambil data modul jika belum ada di cache memori
          loadModuleData(menuId);
        }

        function toggleMobileMenu() {
          const mob = document.getElementById('mobileMenu');
          if (mob) mob.classList.toggle('hidden');
        }

        /**
         * Memuat Data Modul Berdasarkan Menu Terpilih (In-Memory Caching)
         */
        function loadModuleData(menuId) {
          if (menuId === 'dashboard') {
            if (appState.metrics) renderDoughnutChart(appState.metrics);
            refreshDashboardData();
          } else if (menuId === 'wali') {
            if (!appState.cache.wali) fetchWaliData();
            else renderWaliList(appState.cache.wali);
          } else if (menuId === 'siswa') {
            if (!appState.cache.siswa) fetchSiswaData();
            else renderSiswaTable(appState.cache.siswa);
          } else if (menuId === 'absensi') {
            if (!appState.cache.siswa) fetchSiswaData(function () { loadAbsensiForSession(); });
            else loadAbsensiForSession();
            if (!appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi) loadRiwayatAbsensi(false);
          } else if (menuId === 'izin') {
            if (!appState.cache.izin) fetchIzinData();
            else renderIzinTable(appState.cache.izin);
          } else if (menuId === 'penanganan') {
            if (!appState.cache.sakit) fetchSakitData();
            if (!appState.cache.pelanggaran) fetchPelanggaranData();
          } else if (menuId === 'kebutuhan') {
            if (!appState.cache.kebutuhan) fetchKebutuhanInventarisData();
            else renderKebutuhanDanInventarisTables();
          }
        }

        function refreshDashboardData() {
          fetchInitialData();
          showToast('Data dashboard telah diperbarui.', 'info');
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // LOGIKA AUTENTIKASI PENGGUNA (ROLE-BASED ACCESS CONTROL)
        // =====================================================================

        function openModalLogin() {
          document.getElementById('modalLogin').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalLogin() {
          document.getElementById('modalLogin').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitLogin() {
          const u = document.getElementById('loginUsername').value;
          const p = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value;
          const btn = document.getElementById('btnLoginSubmit');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Memverifikasi...';

          callBackend('apiLogin', [u, p], function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Masuk Sekarang';
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.currentUser = res.user;
              localStorage.setItem('esowali_user', JSON.stringify(res.user));
              closeModalLogin();
              updateAuthUI();
              showToast('Selamat datang, ' + res.user.namaLengkap + ' (' + res.user.role + ')!', 'success');
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Login gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Masuk Sekarang';
          });
        }

        function doLogout() {
          appState.currentUser = null;
          localStorage.removeItem('esowali_user');
          updateAuthUI();
          navigateMenu('beranda');
          showToast('Anda telah keluar dari sesi petugas.', 'info');
        }

        function updateAuthUI() {
          const guestBadge = document.getElementById('userBadgeGuest');
          const userBadge = document.getElementById('userBadgeLoggedIn');
          const nameDisp = document.getElementById('userNameDisplay');
          const roleDisp = document.getElementById('userRoleDisplay');
          const isLogged = !!appState.currentUser;
          const role = isLogged ? (appState.currentUser.role || '').toLowerCase() : '';
          const isAdmin = role === 'admin';
          const isAsuh = role.indexOf('asuh') !== -1;
          const isAsrama = role.indexOf('asrama') !== -1;

          if (isLogged) {
            if (guestBadge) {
              guestBadge.classList.add('hidden');
              guestBadge.style.display = 'none';
            }
            if (userBadge) {
              userBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
              userBadge.style.display = 'flex';
            }
            if (nameDisp) nameDisp.innerText = appState.currentUser.namaLengkap;
            if (roleDisp) roleDisp.innerText = appState.currentUser.role;
            const initialEl = document.getElementById('userInitialDisplay');
            if (initialEl) {
              initialEl.innerText = (appState.currentUser.namaLengkap || 'P').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase();
            }
          } else {
            if (guestBadge) {
              guestBadge.classList.remove('hidden');
              guestBadge.style.display = 'flex';
            }
            if (userBadge) {
              userBadge.classList.add('hidden');
              userBadge.style.display = 'none';
            }
          }

          // Pengaturan Visibilitas Menu (Public: Beranda, Dashboard, Wali, Siswa; Auth: Absensi, Izin, Penanganan, Kebutuhan)
          const authMenus = ['absensi', 'izin', 'penanganan', 'kebutuhan'];
          authMenus.forEach(function (m) {
            const dBtn = document.getElementById('nav-' + m);
            const mBtn = document.getElementById('mobnav-' + m);
            if (dBtn) {
              if (isLogged) {
                dBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
              } else {
                dBtn.classList.add('hidden');
              }
            }
            if (mBtn) {
              if (isLogged) {
                mBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
              } else {
                mBtn.classList.add('hidden');
              }
            }
          });

          // Tampilkan tombol aksi sesuai role
          toggleBtn('btnSettingsAdmin', isAdmin);
          toggleBtn('btnKelolaUserAdmin', isAdmin);
          toggleBtn('btnTambahWali', isAdmin);
          toggleBtn('btnTambahSiswa', isAdmin || isAsuh);
          toggleBtn('btnTambahIzin', isAdmin || isAsrama);
          toggleBtn('btnCatatSakit', isAdmin || isAsuh || isAsrama);
          toggleBtn('btnCatatAsesmen', isAdmin || isAsuh);
          toggleBtn('btnTambahKebutuhan', isAdmin || isAsrama);

          // Re-render tabel untuk menyesuaikan tombol aksi yang diizinkan & kolom Aksi/Dokumen
          if (appState.cache.wali) renderWaliList(appState.cache.wali);
          if (appState.cache.siswa) renderSiswaTable(appState.cache.siswa);
          if (appState.cache.izin) renderIzinTable(appState.cache.izin);
        }

        function toggleBtn(id, isShow) {
          const el = document.getElementById(id);
          if (!el) return;
          if (isShow) {
            el.classList.remove('hidden');
            el.classList.add('inline-flex');
          } else {
            el.classList.add('hidden');
            el.classList.remove('inline-flex');
          }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL WALI ASUH & ASRAMA
        // =====================================================================

        function fetchWaliData() {
          callBackend('apiGetWaliList', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.wali = res.data;
              renderWaliList(res.data);
              renderWaliPipeline(res.data);
              populateWaliPiketDropdowns();
            }
          });
        }

        function renderWaliList(list) {
          renderWaliPipeline(list);
          populateWaliPiketDropdowns();
          const grid = document.getElementById('gridWaliList');
          if (!grid) return;
          grid.innerHTML = '';

          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
            grid.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada data Wali Asuh / Asrama.</div>';
            return;
          }

          const isAdmin = appState.currentUser && appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase() === 'admin';

          list.forEach(function (item, idx) {
            const defaultAvatar = item.jenisKelamin === 'Perempuan'
              ? 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/4140/4140047.png'
              : 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png';
            const foto = formatImageUrl(item.fotoUrl, defaultAvatar);
            const isAsuh = (item.jabatan || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('asuh') !== -1;
            const badgeColor = isAsuh
              ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-maroon to-red-700 text-white shadow-xs'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-indigo-800 text-white shadow-xs';
            const topBarColor = isAsuh
              ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-maroon via-red-500 to-amber-500'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 via-indigo-500 to-purple-500';

            const waNumber = (item.noWhatsApp || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
            const cleanWa = waNumber.startsWith('0') ? '62' + waNumber.substring(1) : waNumber;
            const waLink = cleanWa ? 'https://wa.me/' + cleanWa : '#';

            let adminBtn = '';
            if (isAdmin) {
              adminBtn = '<button onclick="editWaliByIndex(' + idx + ')" class="text-xs text-navy hover:text-maroon font-bold flex items-center space-x-1 transition px-2 py-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">' +
                '<svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path></svg>' +
                '<span>Edit</span></button>';
            }

            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-sm p-5 flex flex-col justify-between hover:shadow-2xl hover:-translate-y-1.5 transition-all duration-300 relative overflow-hidden group hover:border-slate-300';
            card.innerHTML =
              '<div class="h-1.5 w-full absolute top-0 left-0 ' + topBarColor + '"></div>' +
              '<div>' +
              '<div class="flex items-center space-x-3.5 pb-3.5 border-b border-slate-100">' +
              '<div class="relative flex-shrink-0">' +
              '<img src="' + foto + '" class="w-14 h-14 object-cover rounded-2xl border-2 border-white shadow-md ring-2 ring-slate-200 group-hover:ring-amber-400 group-hover:scale-105 transition-all duration-300" onerror="this.src=\'' + defaultAvatar + '\'">' +
              '<span class="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 border-2 border-white shadow-xs" title="Pegawai Aktif"></span>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div class="overflow-hidden space-y-1 flex-1">' +
              '<span class="inline-block text-[9px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ' + badgeColor + ' uppercase tracking-wider">' + (item.jabatan || 'Wali Asuh') + '</span>' +
              '<h4 class="font-black text-sm text-slate-900 truncate leading-snug group-hover:text-navy transition-colors" title="' + item.nama + '">' + item.nama + '</h4>' +
              '<div class="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-[10px] text-slate-700 font-mono"><svg class="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-slate-400 flex-shrink-0" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 2a1 1 0 00-1 1v1a1 1 0 002 0V3a1 1 0 00-1-1zM4 4h3a3 3 0 006 0h3a2 2 0 012 2v9a2 2 0 01-2 2H4a2 2 0 01-2-2V6a2 2 0 012-2zm2.5 7a1.5 1.5 0 100-3 1.5 1.5 0 000 3zm2.45 4a2.5 2.5 0 00-4.9 0h4.9zM12 9a1 1 0 100 2h3a1 1 0 100-2h-3zm-1 4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 110 2h-2a1 1 0 01-1-1z" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span>NIP: ' + (item.nip || '-') + '</span></div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div class="pt-3 space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-600">' +
              '<div class="flex items-center justify-between py-1 px-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">' +
              '<span class="text-slate-500 font-medium text-[11px] flex items-center space-x-1"><svg class="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M.057 24l1.687-6.163c-1.041-1.804-1.588-3.849-1.587-5.946.003-6.556 5.338-11.891 11.893-11.891 3.181.001 6.167 1.24 8.413 3.488 2.245 2.248 3.481 5.236 3.48 8.414-.003 6.557-5.338 11.892-11.893 11.892-1.99-.001-3.951-.5-5.688-1.448l-6.305 1.654zm6.597-3.807c1.676.995 3.276 1.591 5.392 1.592 5.448 0 9.886-4.434 9.889-9.885.002-5.462-4.415-9.89-9.881-9.892-5.452 0-9.887 4.434-9.889 9.884-.001 2.225.651 3.891 1.746 5.634l-.999 3.648 3.742-.981z"/></svg><span>WhatsApp</span></span>' +
              '<strong class="text-slate-800 font-mono text-[11px]">' + (item.noWhatsApp || '-') + '</strong>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<p class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500 text-[11px] px-1"><span>TMT SK:</span><strong class="text-slate-700">' + (item.tmtSk || '-') + '</strong></p>' +
              '<p class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500 text-[11px] px-1"><span>Jenis Kelamin:</span><strong class="text-slate-700">' + item.jenisKelamin + '</strong></p>' +
              '</div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div class="pt-3.5 border-t border-slate-100 mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">' +
              (cleanWa ? '<a href="' + waLink + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-black text-xs px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/20 hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 transition-all">' +
                '<svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12.031 6.172c-3.181 0-5.767 2.586-5.768 5.766-.001 1.298.38 2.27 1.019 3.287l-.582 2.128 2.182-.573c.978.58 1.911.928 3.145.929 3.178 0 5.767-2.587 5.768-5.766.001-3.187-2.575-5.77-5.764-5.771zm3.392 8.244c-.144.405-.837.774-1.17.824-.312.045-.694.079-2.115-.508-1.709-.705-2.812-2.45-2.898-2.564-.084-.114-.693-.923-.693-1.758 0-.834.436-1.246.592-1.417.156-.171.341-.214.455-.214.114 0 .228.001.328.006.106.006.248-.04.388.297.144.347.491 1.2.534 1.288.043.088.072.19.014.305-.058.114-.087.185-.173.285-.085.1-.18.223-.257.3-.085.085-.174.177-.075.347.099.171.442.73 1.01 1.236.73.65 1.346.852 1.536.947.19.095.301.079.414-.05.114-.129.489-.571.619-.766.13-.195.26-.163.436-.098.177.065 1.122.529 1.314.625.192.096.32.143.367.224.048.081.048.471-.096.876z"/></svg>' +
                '<span>Chat WhatsApp</span>' +
                '</a>' : '<span class="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No WA tidak ada</span>') +
              adminBtn +
              '</div>';
            grid.appendChild(card);
          });
        }

        function openModalWali() {
          document.getElementById('formWali').reset();
          document.getElementById('waliId').value = '';
          document.getElementById('modalWaliTitle').innerText = 'Tambah Wali Asuh / Asrama Baru';
          document.getElementById('modalWali').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalWali() {
          document.getElementById('modalWali').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function editWaliByIndex(idx) {
          const item = appState.cache.wali[idx];
          if (!item) return;
          document.getElementById('waliId').value = item.id;
          document.getElementById('waliNama').value = item.nama;
          document.getElementById('waliNip').value = item.nip;
          document.getElementById('waliJk').value = item.jenisKelamin;
          document.getElementById('waliJabatan').value = item.jabatan;
          document.getElementById('waliTmtSk').value = item.tmtSk;
          document.getElementById('waliNoWa').value = item.noWhatsApp;
          document.getElementById('waliFotoUrl').value = item.fotoUrl;
          document.getElementById('modalWaliTitle').innerText = 'Edit Data: ' + item.nama;
          document.getElementById('modalWali').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function submitWali() {
          const fileInput = document.getElementById('waliFotoFile');
          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveWali');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          const data = {
            id: document.getElementById('waliId').value,
            nama: document.getElementById('waliNama').value,
            nip: document.getElementById('waliNip').value,
            jenisKelamin: document.getElementById('waliJk').value,
            jabatan: document.getElementById('waliJabatan').value,
            tmtSk: document.getElementById('waliTmtSk').value,
            noWhatsApp: document.getElementById('waliNoWa').value,
            fotoUrl: document.getElementById('waliFotoUrl').value,
            fotoBase64: ''
          };

          if (fileInput && fileInput.files && fileInput.files[0]) {
            btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Foto...';
            compressImageFile(fileInput.files[0], 1000, 1000, 0.78, function (base64) {
              data.fotoBase64 = base64;
              btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';
              sendWaliData(data, btn);
            });
          } else {
            sendWaliData(data, btn);
          }
        }

        function sendWaliData(data, btn) {
          callBackend('apiSaveWali', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Data Wali';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalWali();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              appState.cache.wali = null;
              fetchWaliData();
              fetchInitialData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal menyimpan.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Data Wali';
          });
        }

        // Kelola Akun Kredensial User (Admin)
        function openModalKelolaUser() {
          document.getElementById('formKelolaUser').reset();
          document.getElementById('modalKelolaUser').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function closeModalKelolaUser() {
          document.getElementById('modalKelolaUser').classList.add('hidden');
        }
        function submitKelolaUser() {
          const data = {
            username: document.getElementById('userEditUsername').value,
            password: document.getElementById('userEditPassword').value,
            role: document.getElementById('userEditRole').value,
            status: 'Aktif'
          };
          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveUser');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          callBackend('apiSaveUserCredentials', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Perbarui Sandi';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalKelolaUser();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Perbarui Sandi';
          });
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL SISWA, ID CARD & PORTOFOLIO
        // =====================================================================

        function fetchSiswaData(callback) {
          callBackend('apiGetSiswaList', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.siswa = res.data;
              renderSiswaTable(res.data);
              populateSiswaDropdowns(res.data);
              if (callback) callback();
            }
          });
        }

        function renderSiswaTable(list) {
          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodySiswa');
          const thAksi = document.getElementById('thAksiSiswa');
          if (!tbody) return;
          tbody.innerHTML = '';

          const isLogged = !!appState.currentUser;
          const isAdminOrAsuh = isLogged && (appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase() === 'admin' || appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase().indexOf('asuh') !== -1);

          // Atur visibilitas kolom Aksi / Dokumen di header table (Hanya Tampil Saat Petugas Login)
          if (thAksi) {
            if (isLogged) {
              thAksi.classList.remove('hidden');
            } else {
              thAksi.classList.add('hidden');
            }
          }

          const colSpanCount = isLogged ? 7 : 6;

          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="' + colSpanCount + '" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada data siswa terdaftar.</td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          list.forEach(function (s, idx) {
            const defaultAvatar = s.jenisKelamin === 'Perempuan'
              ? 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/4140/4140047.png'
              : 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png';
            const foto = formatImageUrl(s.fotoUrl, defaultAvatar);
            const isHadir = (s.statusAsrama || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('hadir') !== -1;
            const statusBadge = isHadir
              ? '<span class="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-emerald-200">Hadir di Asrama</span>'
              : '<span class="bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-amber-200">' + s.statusAsrama + '</span>';

            // Kolom Aksi / Dokumen hanya di-render untuk petugas login
            let aksiTd = '';
            if (isLogged) {
              let editBtn = '';
              if (isAdminOrAsuh) {
                editBtn = '<button onclick="editSiswaByIndex(' + idx + ')" title="Edit Siswa" class="p-1.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-lg">' +
                  '<svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path></svg>' +
                  '</button>';
              }
              aksiTd = '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center space-x-1.5">' +
                '<button onclick="previewIdCardByIndex(' + idx + ')" title="Cetak ID Card" class="p-1.5 bg-navy hover:bg-navy-700 text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">' +
                '<svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10 6H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v9a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V8a2 2 0 00-2-2h-5m-4 0V5a2 2 0 114 0v1m-4 0a2 2 0 104 0m-5 8a2 2 0 100-4 2 2 0 000 4zm0 0c1.306 0 2.417.835 2.83 2H9.17A3.001 3.001 0 0112 14z"></path></svg>' +
                '</button>' +
                '<button onclick="previewPortofolioByIndex(' + idx + ')" title="Portofolio Lengkap" class="p-1.5 bg-maroon hover:bg-maroon-700 text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">' +
                '<svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"></path></svg>' +
                '</button>' +
                editBtn +
                '</td>';
            }

            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
            tr.innerHTML =
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 flex items-center space-x-3">' +
              '<img src="' + foto + '" class="w-9 h-9 object-cover rounded-lg border border-slate-200" onerror="this.src=\'' + defaultAvatar + '\'">' +
              '<div><span class="font-bold text-slate-800">' + s.nama + '</span></div>' +
              '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-medium text-slate-600">' + s.nisn + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600">' + (s.jenisKelamin === 'Laki-laki' ? 'L' : 'P') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-medium text-navy">' + (s.noKamar || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600">' + (s.namaWaliAsuh || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3">' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
              aksiTd;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
        }

        function filterSiswaTable() {
          const q = (document.getElementById('searchSiswaInput').value || '').toLowerCase();
          const g = document.getElementById('filterGenderSiswa').value;
          if (!appState.cache.siswa) return;

          const filtered = appState.cache.siswa.filter(function (s) {
            const matchQ = !q || (s.nama && s.nama.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1) ||
              (s.nisn && s.nisn.indexOf(q) !== -1) ||
              (s.noKamar && s.noKamar.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1);
            const matchG = !g || s.jenisKelamin === g;
            return matchQ && matchG;
          });

          renderSiswaTable(filtered);
        }

        function openModalSiswa() {
          document.getElementById('formSiswa').reset();
          document.getElementById('modalSiswaTitle').innerText = 'Tambah Data Siswa(i) Baru';
          document.getElementById('modalSiswa').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalSiswa() {
          document.getElementById('modalSiswa').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function editSiswaByIndex(idx) {
          const s = appState.cache.siswa[idx];
          if (!s) return;
          document.getElementById('sisNama').value = s.nama;
          document.getElementById('sisNisn').value = s.nisn;
          document.getElementById('sisJk').value = s.jenisKelamin;
          document.getElementById('sisTempatLahir').value = s.tempatLahir || '';
          document.getElementById('sisTanggalLahir').value = s.tanggalLahir || '';
          document.getElementById('sisNik').value = s.nik || '';
          document.getElementById('sisNoKamar').value = s.noKamar || '';
          document.getElementById('sisHobi').value = s.hobi || '';
          document.getElementById('sisCitaCita').value = s.citaCita || '';
          document.getElementById('sisEkskul').value = s.ekskul || '';
          document.getElementById('sisPrestasi').value = s.prestasi || '';
          document.getElementById('sisAyah').value = s.ortuAyah || '';
          document.getElementById('sisIbu').value = s.ortuIbu || '';
          document.getElementById('sisNoHpOrtu').value = s.noHpOrtu || '';
          document.getElementById('sisWaliAsuh').value = s.namaWaliAsuh || '';
          document.getElementById('sisAlamat').value = s.alamat || '';
          document.getElementById('sisNoBpjs').value = s.noBpjs || '';
          document.getElementById('sisFaskes').value = s.faskes || '';
          document.getElementById('sisFotoUrl').value = s.fotoUrl || '';

          document.getElementById('modalSiswaTitle').innerText = 'Edit Data: ' + s.nama;
          document.getElementById('modalSiswa').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function submitSiswa() {
          const fileInput = document.getElementById('sisFotoFile');
          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveSiswa');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          const data = {
            nama: document.getElementById('sisNama').value,
            nisn: document.getElementById('sisNisn').value,
            jenisKelamin: document.getElementById('sisJk').value,
            tempatLahir: document.getElementById('sisTempatLahir').value,
            tanggalLahir: document.getElementById('sisTanggalLahir').value,
            nik: document.getElementById('sisNik').value,
            noKamar: document.getElementById('sisNoKamar').value,
            hobi: document.getElementById('sisHobi').value,
            citaCita: document.getElementById('sisCitaCita').value,
            ekskul: document.getElementById('sisEkskul').value,
            prestasi: document.getElementById('sisPrestasi').value,
            ortuAyah: document.getElementById('sisAyah').value,
            ortuIbu: document.getElementById('sisIbu').value,
            noHpOrtu: document.getElementById('sisNoHpOrtu').value,
            namaWaliAsuh: document.getElementById('sisWaliAsuh').value,
            alamat: document.getElementById('sisAlamat').value,
            noBpjs: document.getElementById('sisNoBpjs').value,
            faskes: document.getElementById('sisFaskes').value,
            fotoUrl: document.getElementById('sisFotoUrl').value,
            fotoBase64: ''
          };

          if (fileInput && fileInput.files && fileInput.files[0]) {
            btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Foto...';
            compressImageFile(fileInput.files[0], 1000, 1000, 0.78, function (base64) {
              data.fotoBase64 = base64;
              btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';
              sendSiswaData(data, btn);
            });
          } else {
            sendSiswaData(data, btn);
          }
        }

        function sendSiswaData(data, btn) {
          callBackend('apiSaveSiswa', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Data Siswa';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalSiswa();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              appState.cache.siswa = null;
              fetchSiswaData();
              fetchInitialData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal menyimpan.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Data Siswa';
          });
        }

        // Modal Preview ID Card dengan Generator QR Code
        function previewIdCardByIndex(idx) {
          if (!appState.currentUser) {
            showToast('Aksi cetak ID Card khusus untuk petugas terdaftar. Silakan login.', 'warning');
            openModalLogin();
            return;
          }
          const s = appState.cache.siswa[idx];
          if (!s) return;

          document.getElementById('idCardNama').innerText = s.nama;
          document.getElementById('idCardNisn').innerText = s.nisn;
          document.getElementById('idCardJk').innerText = s.jenisKelamin;
          document.getElementById('idCardKamar').innerText = s.noKamar || '-';

          const defaultSiswaAvatar = s.jenisKelamin === 'Perempuan'
            ? 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/4140/4140047.png'
            : 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png';
          const idCardFotoEl = document.getElementById('idCardFoto');
          if (idCardFotoEl) {
            idCardFotoEl.onerror = function () { this.src = defaultSiswaAvatar; };
            idCardFotoEl.src = formatImageUrl(s.fotoUrl, defaultSiswaAvatar);
          }

          // Generate Barcode QR Code Dinamis (Menampilkan data lengkap siswa saat di-scan)
          const qrBox = document.getElementById('idCardQrContainer');
          qrBox.innerHTML = '';
          if (typeof QRCode !== 'undefined') {
            const qrContent = [
              '=== KTA DIGITAL e-SIWALI ===',
              'Nama: ' + (s.nama || '-'),
              'NISN: ' + (s.nisn || '-'),
              'JK: ' + (s.jenisKelamin || '-'),
              'Kamar: ' + (s.noKamar || '-'),
              'Wali Asuh: ' + (s.namaWaliAsuh || '-'),
              'Status: Siswa Aktif Asrama',
              'Instansi: SRT 1 Sidenreng Rappang'
            ].join('\n');

            new QRCode(qrBox, {
              text: qrContent,
              width: 76,
              height: 76,
              colorDark: '#0F2537',
              colorLight: '#ffffff',
              correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.M
            });
          }

          document.getElementById('modalIdCard').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalIdCard() {
          document.getElementById('modalIdCard').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        /**
         * Mengunduh Tampilan ID Card sebagai Gambar PNG Berkualitas Tinggi
         */
        function downloadIdCard() {
          const card = document.getElementById('idCardContainer');
          if (!card) return;
          if (typeof html2canvas === 'undefined') {
            showToast('Library pembuat gambar sedang dimuat, mohon coba sebentar lagi.', 'error');
            return;
          }
          showToast('Memproses ID Card untuk diunduh...', 'info');
          const nisn = document.getElementById('idCardNisn') ? document.getElementById('idCardNisn').innerText : 'siswa';
          const nama = document.getElementById('idCardNama') ? document.getElementById('idCardNama').innerText : 'kartu';

          html2canvas(card, {
            scale: 3,
            useCORS: true,
            allowTaint: true,
            backgroundColor: null
          }).then(function (canvas) {
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.download = 'ID_CARD_' + nisn + '_' + nama.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_') + '.png';
            link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
            link.click();
            showToast('ID Card berhasil diunduh sebagai gambar PNG!', 'success');
          }).catch(function (err) {
            showToast('Gagal mengunduh ID Card: ' + err.message, 'error');
          });
        }
