        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL ABSENSI 11 SESI & QR SCANNER
        // =====================================================================

        function loadAbsensiForSession() {
          const tgl = document.getElementById('absensiTanggalInput').value;
          const sesi = document.getElementById('absensiSesiSelect').value;
          const d = tgl ? tgl.split('-').reverse().join('/') : '';

          callBackend('apiGetAbsensiData', [d, sesi], function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.absensi = res.data;
              renderAbsensiChecklist(res.data);
            }
          });
        }

        function renderAbsensiChecklist(existingRecords) {
          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodyAbsensiChecklist');
          if (!tbody) return;
          tbody.innerHTML = '';

          if (!appState.cache.siswa || appState.cache.siswa.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="py-6 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada siswa terdaftar.</td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          const mapExisting = {};
          if (existingRecords) {
            existingRecords.forEach(function (r) {
              mapExisting[r.nisn] = r.status;
            });
          }

          appState.cache.siswa.forEach(function (s) {
            const curStatus = mapExisting[s.nisn] || 'Hadir';

            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
            tr.innerHTML =
              '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-800">' + s.nama + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2 px-2 font-mono text-slate-500">' + s.nisn + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2 px-2 text-navy">' + (s.noKamar || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center">' +
              '<div class="inline-flex space-x-2 text-xs">' +
              '<label class="flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="abs_' + s.nisn + '" value="Hadir" ' + (curStatus === 'Hadir' ? 'checked' : '') + ' class="text-emerald-600 focus:ring-emerald-500"><span class="text-emerald-700 font-medium">Hadir</span></label>' +
              '<label class="flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="abs_' + s.nisn + '" value="Sakit" ' + (curStatus === 'Sakit' ? 'checked' : '') + ' class="text-rose-600 focus:ring-rose-500"><span class="text-rose-700 font-medium">Sakit</span></label>' +
              '<label class="flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="abs_' + s.nisn + '" value="Izin" ' + (curStatus === 'Izin' ? 'checked' : '') + ' class="text-amber-600 focus:ring-amber-500"><span class="text-amber-700 font-medium">Izin</span></label>' +
              '<label class="flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"><input type="radio" name="abs_' + s.nisn + '" value="Alpa" ' + (curStatus === 'Alpa' ? 'checked' : '') + ' class="text-slate-600 focus:ring-slate-500"><span class="text-slate-700 font-medium">Alpa</span></label>' +
              '</div>' +
              '</td>';
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
        }

        function markAllAbsensiHadir() {
          if (!appState.cache.siswa) return;
          appState.cache.siswa.forEach(function (s) {
            const radios = document.getElementsByName('abs_' + s.nisn);
            radios.forEach(function (r) {
              if (r.value === 'Hadir') r.checked = true;
            });
          });
          showToast('Semua siswa ditandai Hadir.', 'info');
        }

        function simpanAbsensiBatch() {
          if (!appState.cache.siswa) return;
          const tgl = document.getElementById('absensiTanggalInput').value;
          const sesi = document.getElementById('absensiSesiSelect').value;
          const d = tgl ? tgl.split('-').reverse().join('/') : '';
          const petugas = appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Piket Asrama';

          const records = [];
          appState.cache.siswa.forEach(function (s) {
            const radios = document.getElementsByName('abs_' + s.nisn);
            let selected = 'Hadir';
            radios.forEach(function (r) {
              if (r.checked) selected = r.value;
            });
            records.push({
              nisn: s.nisn,
              nama: s.nama,
              noKamar: s.noKamar || '-',
              status: selected
            });
          });

          callBackend('apiSaveAbsensiBatch', [records, sesi, d, petugas], function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              loadAbsensiForSession();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal simpan absensi.', 'error');
            }
          });
        }

        // Pemindai QR Code via html5-qrcode
        function triggerNativeQrCapture() {
          const inp = document.getElementById('nativeQrCaptureInput');
          if (inp) inp.click();
        }

        function scanQrFromNativeCapture(input) {
          if (!input.files || !input.files[0]) return;
          const file = input.files[0];
          showToast('Membaca barcode QR hasil jepretan kamera...', 'info');

          if (typeof Html5Qrcode === 'undefined') {
            showToast('Library pemindai QR belum selesai dimuat.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          const qrScanner = new Html5Qrcode('qrReader');
          qrScanner.scanFile(file, true)
            .then(function (decodedText) {
              handleQrScanSuccess(decodedText);
              input.value = '';
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
              showToast('Kode QR tidak terdeteksi pada foto. Pastikan barcode terlihat jelas dan jernih.', 'error');
              input.value = '';
            });
        }

        function startQrScanner() {
          const container = document.getElementById('qrReaderContainer');
          const placeholder = document.getElementById('qrPlaceholder');
          if (placeholder) placeholder.classList.add('hidden');

          if (typeof Html5Qrcode === 'undefined') {
            showToast('Library scanner QR sedang dimuat. Coba beberapa saat lagi.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          if (!appState.html5QrCode) {
            appState.html5QrCode = new Html5Qrcode('qrReader');
          }

          document.getElementById('qrStatusBadge').innerText = 'Memindai...';
          document.getElementById('qrStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold animate-pulse';

          appState.html5QrCode.start(
            { facingMode: 'environment' },
            { fps: 10, qrbox: { width: 200, height: 200 } },
            function (decodedText) {
              handleQrScanSuccess(decodedText);
            },
            function (errorMessage) {
              // ignore stream tick errors
            }
          ).catch(function (err) {
            console.warn('Live camera stream warning:', err);
            const errStr = (err + '').toLowerCase();
            if (errStr.indexOf('notallowederror') !== -1 || errStr.indexOf('permission') !== -1) {
              showToast('Izin kamera live dibatasi oleh peramban/iframe Google. Membuka kamera HP langsung...', 'warning');
              setTimeout(function () {
                triggerNativeQrCapture();
              }, 600);
            } else {
              showToast('Kamera live tidak dapat dibuka: ' + err + '. Silakan gunakan tombol Jepret Kamera HP.', 'error');
            }
            stopQrScanner();
          });
        }

        function stopQrScanner() {
          if (appState.html5QrCode) {
            appState.html5QrCode.stop().then(function () {
              appState.html5QrCode.clear();
              appState.html5QrCode = null;
            }).catch(function () { });
          }
          const placeholder = document.getElementById('qrPlaceholder');
          if (placeholder) placeholder.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('qrStatusBadge').innerText = 'Siap';
          document.getElementById('qrStatusBadge').className = 'text-[10px] bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold';
        }

        /**
         * Ekstraktor NISN Cerdas dari QR Code
         * Mampu mengenali QR Code berkonten teks penuh KTA mau pun barcode NISN numerik polos
         */
        function extractNisnFromQr(rawText) {
          if (!rawText) return '';
          const text = rawText.toString().trim();

          // 1. Cek format terstruktur: "NISN: 1234567890" atau "NISN : 1234567890"
          const matchNisn = text.match(/NISN\s*:\s*([0-9a-zA-Z\-_]+)/i);
          if (matchNisn && matchNisn[1]) {
            return matchNisn[1].trim();
          }

          // 2. Jika seluruh string adalah angka 8-16 digit (NISN standar)
          const matchPure = text.match(/^\d{8,16}$/);
          if (matchPure) {
            return matchPure[0];
          }

          // 3. Fallback: Cari deretan angka 8-16 digit pertama dalam teks
          const matchAnyDigits = text.match(/\b\d{8,16}\b/);
          if (matchAnyDigits) {
            return matchAnyDigits[0];
          }

          // 4. Jika tidak cocok, kembalikan baris pertama bersih
          return text.split('\n')[0].trim();
        }

        /**
         * Membunyikan Nada Beep Sukses Presensi Menggunakan Web Audio API
         */
        function playSuccessBeep() {
          try {
            const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
            if (!AudioContext) return;
            const ctx = new AudioContext();
            const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = ctx.createGain();

            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime); // 880 Hz (A5)
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.18, ctx.currentTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.2);

            osc.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(ctx.destination);

            osc.start();
            osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.2);
          } catch (e) {
            // Abaikan jika browser membatasi Web Audio tanpa user-gesture
          }
        }

        function handleQrScanSuccess(rawDecodedText) {
          const cleanNisn = extractNisnFromQr(rawDecodedText);
          if (!cleanNisn) {
            showToast('Format QR Code tidak memuat data NISN siswa yang valid.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          const sesi = document.getElementById('absensiSesiSelect').value;
          const tgl = document.getElementById('absensiTanggalInput').value;
          const d = tgl ? tgl.split('-').reverse().join('/') : '';
          const petugas = appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Piket Asrama';

          showToast('Memverifikasi QR Siswa (NISN: ' + cleanNisn + ')...', 'info');

          callBackend('apiScanQRAbsensi', [cleanNisn, sesi, d, petugas], function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              playSuccessBeep();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');

              // Update radio button checklist sesi aktif secara optimistic
              const radios = document.getElementsByName('abs_' + cleanNisn);
              radios.forEach(function (r) {
                if (r.value === 'Hadir') r.checked = true;
              });

              // Update atau tambahkan data ke cache riwayat absensi secara live
              if (res.data) {
                if (!appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi) {
                  appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi = [];
                }
                // Hapus entri sesi & tanggal yang sama jika ada sebelumnya
                appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi = appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi.filter(function (item) {
                  return !(item.nisn === res.data.nisn && item.tanggal === res.data.tanggal && item.sesi === res.data.sesi);
                });
                // Sisipkan ke urutan paling atas
                appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi.unshift(res.data);
                // Render ulang tabel riwayat jika subtab riwayat aktif
                const subtabRiwayat = document.getElementById('subtabAbsensiRiwayat');
                if (subtabRiwayat && !subtabRiwayat.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                  filterRiwayatAbsensi();
                }
              }
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'QR Siswa tidak terdaftar dalam database.', 'error');
            }
          });
        }

        function scanQrFromFile(event) {
          const file = event.target.files[0];
          if (!file) return;

          const html5QrCode = new Html5Qrcode('qrReader');
          html5QrCode.scanFile(file, true)
            .then(function (decodedText) {
              handleQrScanSuccess(decodedText);
              event.target.value = '';
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
              showToast('Gambar QR Code tidak terbaca: ' + err, 'error');
              event.target.value = '';
            });
        }

        function submitManualNisnAbsensi() {
          const inp = document.getElementById('manualNisnInput');
          const nisn = inp ? inp.value.trim() : '';
          if (!nisn) {
            showToast('Masukkan NISN terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }
          handleQrScanSuccess(nisn);
          inp.value = '';
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // SUB-MODUL REKAP RIWAYAT KEGIATAN SISWA (FILTER REAL-TIME & CETAK)
        // =====================================================================

        function switchAbsensiSubTab(tabName) {
          const tabCatat = document.getElementById('tabAbsensiCatat');
          const tabRiwayat = document.getElementById('tabAbsensiRiwayat');
          const subCatat = document.getElementById('subtabAbsensiCatat');
          const subRiwayat = document.getElementById('subtabAbsensiRiwayat');
          const btnBatch = document.getElementById('btnSimpanAbsensiBatch');

          if (tabName === 'catat') {
            tabCatat.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-navy border-b-2 border-navy transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1.5';
            tabRiwayat.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1.5';
            subCatat.classList.remove('hidden');
            subRiwayat.classList.add('hidden');
            if (btnBatch) btnBatch.classList.remove('hidden');
          } else {
            tabRiwayat.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-navy border-b-2 border-navy transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1.5';
            tabCatat.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1.5';
            subRiwayat.classList.remove('hidden');
            subCatat.classList.add('hidden');
            if (btnBatch) btnBatch.classList.add('hidden');

            // Muat data riwayat bila belum tersedia
            loadRiwayatAbsensi(false);
          }
        }

        function loadRiwayatAbsensi(force) {
          if (!force && appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi && appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi.length > 0) {
            filterRiwayatAbsensi();
            return;
          }

          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodyRiwayatAbsensi');
          if (tbody) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">' +
              '<div class="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2">' +
              '<svg class="animate-spin h-5 w-5 text-navy" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8v8H4z"></path></svg>' +
              '<span>Memuat seluruh riwayat kegiatan siswa...</span>' +
              '</div></td></tr>';
          }

          callBackend('apiGetAbsensiData', ['SEMUA', 'SEMUA'], function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi = res.data || [];
              filterRiwayatAbsensi();
            } else {
              showToast('Gagal memuat riwayat kegiatan: ' + (res ? res.message : 'Error'), 'error');
              if (tbody) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-6 text-center text-rose-500 font-medium">Gagal memuat riwayat kegiatan. Silakan coba kembali.</td></tr>';
              }
            }
          });
        }

        function filterRiwayatAbsensi() {
          const records = appState.cache.riwayatAbsensi || [];
          const tglVal = document.getElementById('filterRiwayatTanggal') ? document.getElementById('filterRiwayatTanggal').value : '';
          const filterTgl = tglVal ? tglVal.split('-').reverse().join('/') : '';
          const filterSesi = (document.getElementById('filterRiwayatSesi') ? document.getElementById('filterRiwayatSesi').value : '').trim();
          const filterStatus = (document.getElementById('filterRiwayatStatus') ? document.getElementById('filterRiwayatStatus').value : '').trim();
          const filterSearch = (document.getElementById('filterRiwayatSearch') ? document.getElementById('filterRiwayatSearch').value : '').toLowerCase().trim();

          const filtered = records.filter(function (r) {
            // 1. Filter Tanggal
            if (filterTgl && r.tanggal !== filterTgl) return false;
            // 2. Filter Sesi
            if (filterSesi && r.sesi !== filterSesi) return false;
            // 3. Filter Status
            if (filterStatus && (r.status || '').toLowerCase() !== filterStatus.toLowerCase()) return false;
            // 4. Pencarian Teks Bebas (Nama, NISN, Kamar)
            if (filterSearch) {
              const nm = (r.nama || '').toLowerCase();
              const ni = (r.nisn || '').toLowerCase();
              const km = (r.noKamar || '').toLowerCase();
              if (nm.indexOf(filterSearch) === -1 && ni.indexOf(filterSearch) === -1 && km.indexOf(filterSearch) === -1) {
                return false;
              }
            }
            return true;
          });

          renderRiwayatAbsensiTable(filtered);
        }

        function clearFilterTanggalRiwayat() {
          const inp = document.getElementById('filterRiwayatTanggal');
          if (inp) inp.value = '';
          filterRiwayatAbsensi();
        }

        function resetFilterRiwayatAbsensi() {
          const tgl = document.getElementById('filterRiwayatTanggal');
          const sesi = document.getElementById('filterRiwayatSesi');
          const status = document.getElementById('filterRiwayatStatus');
          const search = document.getElementById('filterRiwayatSearch');
          if (tgl) tgl.value = '';
          if (sesi) sesi.value = '';
          if (status) status.value = '';
          if (search) search.value = '';
          filterRiwayatAbsensi();
          showToast('Filter riwayat kegiatan telah direset.', 'info');
        }

        function renderRiwayatAbsensiTable(records) {
          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodyRiwayatAbsensi');
          if (!tbody) return;
          tbody.innerHTML = '';

          // Update 5 Statistik Cards
          let cTotal = records.length;
          let cHadir = 0, cSakit = 0, cIzin = 0, cAlpa = 0;

          records.forEach(function (r) {
            const s = (r.status || '').toLowerCase();
            if (s === 'hadir') cHadir++;
            else if (s === 'sakit') cSakit++;
            else if (s === 'izin') cIzin++;
            else if (s === 'alpa') cAlpa++;
          });

          setEl('statRiwayatTotal', cTotal);
          setEl('statRiwayatHadir', cHadir);
          setEl('statRiwayatSakit', cSakit);
          setEl('statRiwayatIzin', cIzin);
          setEl('statRiwayatAlpa', cAlpa);

          if (!records || records.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400">' +
              '<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-1">' +
              '<svg class="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"></path></svg>' +
              '<span class="font-medium text-xs text-slate-500">Tidak ada riwayat kegiatan siswa yang sesuai filter.</span>' +
              '</div></td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          records.forEach(function (item, idx) {
            let badgeStatus = '';
            const st = (item.status || 'Hadir').toLowerCase();
            if (st === 'hadir') {
              badgeStatus = '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200">Hadir</span>';
            } else if (st === 'sakit') {
              badgeStatus = '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border border-rose-200">Sakit</span>';
            } else if (st === 'izin') {
              badgeStatus = '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">Izin</span>';
            } else {
              badgeStatus = '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300">Alpa</span>';
            }

            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition border-b border-slate-100';
            tr.innerHTML =
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-center font-bold text-slate-400">' + (idx + 1) + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3">' +
              '<div class="font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs">' + (item.tanggal || '-') + '</div>' +
              '<span class="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-navy-50 text-navy border border-navy-100">' + (item.sesi || '-') + '</span>' +
              '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-900">' + (item.nama || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 font-mono text-slate-500 text-xs">' + (item.nisn || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 text-navy font-medium text-xs">' + (item.noKamar || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">' + badgeStatus + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-500 text-[11px] font-mono">' + (item.waktuCatat || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600 text-xs font-medium">' + (item.petugas || 'Piket') + '</td>';
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
        }

        function printRiwayatKegiatan() {
          printElement('printAreaRiwayatAbsensi');
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL SURAT IZIN PULANG DAN KELUAR
        // =====================================================================

        function fetchIzinData() {
          callBackend('apiGetIzinList', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.izin = res.data;
              renderIzinTable(res.data);
            }
          });
        }

        function switchIzinSubTab(tab) {
          appState.izinSubTab = tab;
          const tAktif = document.getElementById('tabIzinAktif');
          const tRiwayat = document.getElementById('tabIzinRiwayat');

          if (tab === 'aktif') {
            tAktif.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-maroon border-b-2 border-maroon';
            tRiwayat.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800';
          } else {
            tRiwayat.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-maroon border-b-2 border-maroon';
            tAktif.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800';
          }

          if (appState.cache.izin) renderIzinTable(appState.cache.izin);
        }

        function renderIzinTable(list) {
          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodyIzin');
          if (!tbody) return;
          tbody.innerHTML = '';

          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada catatan surat izin.</td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          const isAktifTab = appState.izinSubTab === 'aktif';
          const filtered = list.filter(function (i) {
            const isAktif = (i.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('aktif') !== -1;
            return isAktifTab ? isAktif : !isAktif;
          });

          if (filtered.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Tidak ada data siswa pada kategori ini.</td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          const isAsramaOrAdmin = appState.currentUser && (appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase() === 'admin' || appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase().indexOf('asrama') !== -1);

          filtered.forEach(function (item, idx) {
            const isAktif = (item.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('aktif') !== -1;
            const statusBadge = isAktif
              ? '<span class="bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-amber-200 animate-pulse">Aktif di Luar</span>'
              : '<span class="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-emerald-200">Sudah Kembali</span>';

            let actionVerif = '';
            if (isAktif && isAsramaOrAdmin) {
              actionVerif = '<button onclick="openVerifikasiKembaliModal(\'' + item.noSurat + '\')" title="Ubah status izin menjadi Hadir di Asrama" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm transition inline-flex items-center space-x-1"><svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path></svg><span>Ubah Hadir</span></button>';
            }

            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
            tr.innerHTML =
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-navy">' + item.noSurat + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-medium text-slate-800">' + item.jenisIzin + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-bold text-slate-900">' + item.nama + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600 text-xs">' + item.waktuIzin + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-red-600 font-medium text-xs">' + item.batasWaktu + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-700">' + item.namaPenjemput + ' (' + item.hubungan + ')</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3">' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center space-x-1.5">' +
              '<button onclick="previewSuratIzinByNoSurat(\'' + item.noSurat + '\')" title="Lihat & Cetak Surat Izin Resmi" class="px-2 py-1 bg-navy hover:bg-navy-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-medium inline-flex items-center space-x-1"><svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"></path></svg><span>Cetak Surat</span></button>' +
              actionVerif +
              '</td>';
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
        }

        function populateSiswaDropdowns(list) {
          const dropIzin = document.getElementById('izinSiswaSelect');
          const dropSakit = document.getElementById('sakitSiswaSelect');
          const dropAsesmen = document.getElementById('asesmenSiswaSelect');

          if (dropIzin) {
            dropIzin.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Nama Siswa(i) --</option>';
            list.forEach(function (s) {
              dropIzin.innerHTML += '<option value="' + s.nisn + '">' + s.nama + ' (' + s.nisn + ' - ' + (s.noKamar || '') + ')</option>';
            });
          }
          if (dropSakit) {
            dropSakit.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Nama Siswa(i) --</option>';
            list.forEach(function (s) {
              dropSakit.innerHTML += '<option value="' + s.nisn + '">' + s.nama + ' (' + s.nisn + ')</option>';
            });
          }
          if (dropAsesmen) {
            dropAsesmen.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Nama Siswa(i) --</option>';
            list.forEach(function (s) {
              dropAsesmen.innerHTML += '<option value="' + s.nisn + '">' + s.nama + ' (' + s.nisn + ')</option>';
            });
          }
        }

        function autoFillSiswaIzin() {
          const nisn = document.getElementById('izinSiswaSelect').value;
          if (!appState.cache.siswa) return;
          const s = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (item) { return item.nisn === nisn; });

          if (s) {
            document.getElementById('izinAutoNisn').innerText = s.nisn;
            document.getElementById('izinAutoJk').innerText = s.jenisKelamin;
            document.getElementById('izinAutoKamar').innerText = s.noKamar || '-';
          } else {
            document.getElementById('izinAutoNisn').innerText = '-';
            document.getElementById('izinAutoJk').innerText = '-';
            document.getElementById('izinAutoKamar').innerText = '-';
          }
        }

        function getFormattedCurrentTime() {
          const now = new Date();
          const pad = function (n) { return n < 10 ? '0' + n : n; };
          return pad(now.getDate()) + '/' + pad(now.getMonth() + 1) + '/' + now.getFullYear() + ' ' +
            pad(now.getHours()) + ':' + pad(now.getMinutes()) + ' WITA';
        }

            container.innerHTML =
              '<div class="p-2.5 bg-sky-50/70 border border-sky-200 rounded-xl space-y-1 text-xs mb-1">' +

              '<span class="font-bold text-sky-800 bg-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-sky-200">Izin Keluar Ringkas</span>' +

              '<div class="flex justify-between items-center">' +

              '<span class="font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 bg-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-sky-200">' + realtimeNow + '</span>' +
              '</div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div>' +
              '<label class="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Batas Tanggal & Waktu Izin (Diisi Manual) *</label>' +
              '<input type="datetime-local" id="izinBatasWaktu" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white focus:ring-1 focus:ring-navy">' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">' +
              '<div>' +
              '<label class="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Nama Penjemput / Pengantar *</label>' +
              '<input type="text" id="izinPenjemput" required placeholder="Nama pengantar / pendamping" class="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg">' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div>' +
              '<label class="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Hubungan dengan Penjemput/Pengantar *</label>' +
              '<select id="izinHubungan" required class="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white">' +
              '<option value="Orang Tua">Orang Tua</option>' +
              '<option value="Saudara/Sepupu">Saudara/Sepupu</option>' +
              '<option value="Kakek/Nenek">Kakek/Nenek</option>' +
              '<option value="Paman/Tante">Paman/Tante</option>' +
              '<option value="Stakeholder SRT">Stakeholder SRT</option>' +
              '</select>' +
              '</div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div>' +
              '<label class="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Upload Foto Siswa Izin Keluar *</label>' +
              '<input type="file" id="izinFoto1" accept="image/*" required class="w-full text-xs text-slate-500 file:mr-2 file:py-1 file:px-3 file:rounded-md file:border-0 file:text-xs file:font-semibold file:bg-slate-100 file:text-slate-700 hover:file:bg-slate-200">' +
              '</div>';
          }
        }

        /**
         * Mengisi Pilihan Dropdown Wali Asuh Piket & Wali Asrama Piket pada Formulir Surat Izin
         */
        function populateWaliPiketDropdowns() {
          const dropAsuh = document.getElementById('izinWaliAsuhPiket');
          const dropAsrama = document.getElementById('izinWaliAsramaPiket');
          if (!dropAsuh && !dropAsrama) return;

          const list = appState.cache.wali || [];

          if (dropAsuh) {
            dropAsuh.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Wali Asuh Piket --</option>';
            list.forEach(function (w) {
              const isAsuh = (w.jabatan || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('asuh') !== -1;
              const opt = document.createElement('option');
              opt.value = w.nama + ' (' + (w.jabatan || 'Wali Asuh') + ')';
              opt.innerText = w.nama + ' (' + (w.jabatan || 'Wali Asuh') + ')';
              if (isAsuh && dropAsuh.options.length === 1) opt.selected = true;
              dropAsuh.appendChild(opt);
            });
          }

          if (dropAsrama) {
            dropAsrama.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Wali Asrama Piket --</option>';
            list.forEach(function (w) {
              const isAsrama = (w.jabatan || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('asrama') !== -1;
              const opt = document.createElement('option');
              opt.value = w.nama + ' (' + (w.jabatan || 'Wali Asrama') + ')';
              opt.innerText = w.nama + ' (' + (w.jabatan || 'Wali Asrama') + ')';
              if (isAsrama && dropAsrama.options.length === 1) opt.selected = true;
              dropAsrama.appendChild(opt);
            });
          }
        }

        function openModalTambahIzin() {
          document.getElementById('formTambahIzin').reset();
          renderDynamicIzinFields();
          autoFillSiswaIzin();
          populateWaliPiketDropdowns();
          document.getElementById('modalTambahIzin').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalTambahIzin() {
          document.getElementById('modalTambahIzin').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitTambahIzin() {
          const nisn = document.getElementById('izinSiswaSelect').value;
          if (!nisn) {
            showToast('Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }
          const s = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (item) { return item.nisn === nisn; });
          if (!s) return;

          const batasWaktuRaw = document.getElementById('izinBatasWaktu') ? document.getElementById('izinBatasWaktu').value : '';
          if (!batasWaktuRaw) {
            showToast('Tentukan batas tanggal & waktu izin terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          let formattedBatasWaktu = batasWaktuRaw;
          if (batasWaktuRaw.indexOf('T') !== -1) {
            const parts = batasWaktuRaw.split('T');
            const dParts = parts[0].split('-');
            formattedBatasWaktu = dParts[2] + '/' + dParts[1] + '/' + dParts[0] + ' ' + parts[1] + ' WITA';
          }

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitIzin');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menerbitkan...';

          const payload = {
            jenisIzin: document.getElementById('izinJenisSelect').value,
            nisn: s.nisn,
            nama: s.nama,
            jenisKelamin: s.jenisKelamin,
            noKamar: s.noKamar || '-',
            batasWaktu: formattedBatasWaktu,
            namaPenjemput: document.getElementById('izinPenjemput') ? document.getElementById('izinPenjemput').value : '-',
            hubungan: document.getElementById('izinHubungan') ? document.getElementById('izinHubungan').value : '-',
            waliAsuhPiket: document.getElementById('izinWaliAsuhPiket') ? document.getElementById('izinWaliAsuhPiket').value : '-',
            waliAsramaPiket: document.getElementById('izinWaliAsramaPiket') ? document.getElementById('izinWaliAsramaPiket').value : '-',
            fotoBukti1Base64: '',
            fotoBukti2Base64: '',
            petugas: appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Wali Asrama'
          };

          const file1 = document.getElementById('izinFoto1');
          const file2 = document.getElementById('izinFoto2');

          const processFoto2 = function () {
            if (file2 && file2.files && file2.files[0]) {
              btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Foto 2...';
              compressImageFile(file2.files[0], 1000, 1000, 0.78, function (base64_2) {
                payload.fotoBukti2Base64 = base64_2;
                btn.innerText = 'Menerbitkan...';
                sendIzinPayload(payload, btn);
              });
            } else {
              btn.innerText = 'Menerbitkan...';
              sendIzinPayload(payload, btn);
            }
          };

          if (file1 && file1.files && file1.files[0]) {
            btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Foto 1...';
            compressImageFile(file1.files[0], 1000, 1000, 0.78, function (base64_1) {
              payload.fotoBukti1Base64 = base64_1;
              processFoto2();
            });
          } else {
            processFoto2();
          }
        }

        function sendIzinPayload(payload, btn) {
          callBackend('apiSaveIzin', payload, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Terbitkan & Simpan';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalTambahIzin();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              if (res.metrics) {
                appState.metrics = res.metrics;
                renderMetrics(res.metrics);
              }
              appState.cache.izin = null;
              appState.cache.siswa = null;
              fetchIzinData();
              fetchSiswaData();
              fetchInitialData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Terbitkan & Simpan';
          });
        }

        function openVerifikasiKembaliModal(noSurat) {
          const item = appState.cache.izin ? appState.cache.izin.find(function (i) { return i.noSurat === noSurat; }) : null;
          document.getElementById('verifNoSurat').value = noSurat;
          document.getElementById('verifNoSuratLabel').innerText = noSurat;
          if (document.getElementById('verifNamaSiswaLabel')) {
            document.getElementById('verifNamaSiswaLabel').innerText = item ? item.nama : '-';
          }
          if (document.getElementById('verifWaktuRealtimeLabel')) {
            document.getElementById('verifWaktuRealtimeLabel').innerText = getFormattedCurrentTime();
          }
          const fileInput = document.getElementById('verifFotoFile');
          if (fileInput) fileInput.value = '';
          document.getElementById('modalVerifikasiKembali').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalVerifikasiKembali() {
          document.getElementById('modalVerifikasiKembali').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitVerifikasiKembali() {
          const noSurat = document.getElementById('verifNoSurat').value;
          const fileInput = document.getElementById('verifFotoFile');
          if (!fileInput || !fileInput.files || !fileInput.files[0]) {
            showToast('Unggah foto bukti kedatangan fisik terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveVerifikasi');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Foto...';

          compressImageFile(fileInput.files[0], 1000, 1000, 0.78, function (fotoBase64) {
            btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';
            const petugas = appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Piket Asrama';

            callBackend('apiVerifikasiKedatangan', [noSurat, fotoBase64, petugas], function (res) {
              btn.disabled = false;
              btn.innerText = 'Simpan & Ubah Hadir di Asrama';
              if (res && res.success) {
                closeModalVerifikasiKembali();
                showToast(res.message, 'success');
                if (res.metrics) {
                  appState.metrics = res.metrics;
                  renderMetrics(res.metrics);
                }
                appState.cache.izin = null;
                appState.cache.siswa = null;
                fetchIzinData();
                fetchSiswaData();
                fetchInitialData();
              } else {
                showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal verifikasi.', 'error');
              }
            }, function () {
              btn.disabled = false;
              btn.innerText = 'Simpan & Ubah Hadir di Asrama';
            });
          });
        }

        function previewSuratIzinByNoSurat(noSurat) {
          const item = appState.cache.izin.find(function (i) { return i.noSurat === noSurat; });
          if (!item) return;

          document.getElementById('suratNoSurat').innerText = item.noSurat;
          document.getElementById('suratNamaSiswa').innerText = item.nama;
          document.getElementById('suratNisnKamar').innerText = item.nisn + ' / ' + (item.jenisKelamin || '-') + ' / ' + (item.noKamar || '-');
          document.getElementById('suratJenis').innerText = item.jenisIzin;
          document.getElementById('suratWaktuKeluar').innerText = item.waktuIzin;
          document.getElementById('suratBatasKembali').innerText = item.batasWaktu;
          document.getElementById('suratPenjemput').innerText = (item.namaPenjemput || '-') + ' (' + (item.hubungan || '-') + ')';
          document.getElementById('suratTanggalCetak').innerText = item.waktuIzin ? item.waktuIzin.split(' ')[0] : '-';
          document.getElementById('suratTtdPenjemput').innerText = '( ' + (item.namaPenjemput || 'Penjemput') + ' )';
          if (document.getElementById('suratTtdWaliAsuh')) {
            document.getElementById('suratTtdWaliAsuh').innerText = '( ' + (item.waliAsuhPiket || 'Wali Asuh Piket') + ' )';
          }
          document.getElementById('suratTtdPetugas').innerText = '( ' + (item.waliAsramaPiket || item.petugas || 'Wali Asrama Piket') + ' )';

          const fBox = document.getElementById('suratFotoContainer');
          fBox.innerHTML = '';
          if (item.fotoBukti1) {
            const f1Url = formatImageUrl(item.fotoBukti1);
            fBox.innerHTML += '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mb-1">Foto Identitas / Bukti</span><img src="' + f1Url + '" class="h-28 mx-auto rounded border border-slate-300 object-cover" onerror="this.parentElement.style.display=\'none\'"></div>';
          }
          if (item.fotoBukti2) {
            const f2Url = formatImageUrl(item.fotoBukti2);
            fBox.innerHTML += '<div class="text-center"><span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mb-1">Foto Bersama Penjemput</span><img src="' + f2Url + '" class="h-28 mx-auto rounded border border-slate-300 object-cover" onerror="this.parentElement.style.display=\'none\'"></div>';
          }

          document.getElementById('modalCetakSuratIzin').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalCetakSuratIzin() {
          document.getElementById('modalCetakSuratIzin').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL PENANGANAN KHUSUS (SISWA SAKIT & ASESMEN)
        // =====================================================================

        function switchPenangananSubTab(tab) {
          appState.penangananSubTab = tab;
          const tSakit = document.getElementById('tabSakit');
          const tAsesmen = document.getElementById('tabAsesmen');
          const pSakit = document.getElementById('subtab-sakit');
          const pAsesmen = document.getElementById('subtab-asesmen');

          if (tab === 'sakit') {
            tSakit.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-rose-700 border-b-2 border-rose-700';
            tAsesmen.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800';
            pSakit.classList.remove('hidden');
            pAsesmen.classList.add('hidden');
          } else {
            tAsesmen.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 border-b-2 border-slate-800';
            tSakit.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800';
            pAsesmen.classList.remove('hidden');
            pSakit.classList.add('hidden');
          }
        }

        function fetchSakitData() {
          callBackend('apiGetSakitList', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.sakit = res.data;
              renderSakitTable(res.data);
            }
          });
        }

        function renderSakitTable(list) {
          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodySakit');
          if (!tbody) return;
          tbody.innerHTML = '';

          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Tidak ada siswa yang sedang sakit.</td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          list.forEach(function (item) {
            const isSakit = (item.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('sakit') !== -1;
            const statusBadge = isSakit
              ? '<span class="bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-rose-200 animate-pulse">Sakit di Asrama</span>'
              : '<span class="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-emerald-200">Sembuh/Sehat</span>';

            let btnSembuh = '';
            if (isSakit) {
              btnSembuh = '<button onclick="tandaiSembuhById(\'' + item.id + '\')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm transition">Tandai Sembuh</button>';
            }

            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
            tr.innerHTML =
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-bold text-slate-900">' + item.nama + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 font-medium text-navy">' + (item.noKamar || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 text-slate-600 text-xs">' + item.noBpjs + '<br><span class="text-slate-400">' + item.faskes + '</span></td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-rose-700">' + item.suhu + '°C / ' + item.tensi + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-medium text-slate-800">' + item.keluhan + ' (' + item.lamaSakit + ')</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600 text-xs">' + item.obat + '<br><span class="text-slate-400">' + item.rtl + '</span></td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2">' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">' + btnSembuh + '</td>';
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
        }

        function autoFillSiswaSakit() {
          const nisn = document.getElementById('sakitSiswaSelect').value;
          if (!appState.cache.siswa) return;
          const s = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (item) { return item.nisn === nisn; });

          if (s) {
            document.getElementById('sakitAutoNisnKamar').innerText = s.nisn + ' / ' + (s.noKamar || '-');
            if (document.getElementById('sakitAutoJk')) document.getElementById('sakitAutoJk').innerText = s.jenisKelamin || '-';
            document.getElementById('sakitAutoBpjsFaskes').innerText = (s.noBpjs || '-') + ' (' + (s.faskes || '-') + ')';
          } else {
            document.getElementById('sakitAutoNisnKamar').innerText = '-';
            if (document.getElementById('sakitAutoJk')) document.getElementById('sakitAutoJk').innerText = '-';
            document.getElementById('sakitAutoBpjsFaskes').innerText = '-';
          }
        }

        function openModalCatatSakit() {
          document.getElementById('formCatatSakit').reset();
          autoFillSiswaSakit();
          document.getElementById('modalCatatSakit').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalCatatSakit() {
          document.getElementById('modalCatatSakit').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitCatatSakit() {
          const nisn = document.getElementById('sakitSiswaSelect').value;
          if (!nisn) {
            showToast('Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }
          const s = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (item) { return item.nisn === nisn; });
          if (!s) return;

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveSakit');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          const data = {
            nisn: s.nisn,
            nama: s.nama,
            jenisKelamin: s.jenisKelamin,
            noKamar: s.noKamar || '-',
            noBpjs: s.noBpjs || '-',
            faskes: s.faskes || '-',
            tb: document.getElementById('sakitTb').value,
            bb: document.getElementById('sakitBb').value,
            suhu: document.getElementById('sakitSuhu').value,
            tensi: document.getElementById('sakitTensi').value,
            keluhan: document.getElementById('sakitKeluhan').value,
            lamaSakit: document.getElementById('sakitLama').value,
            penanganan: document.getElementById('sakitPenanganan').value,
            obat: document.getElementById('sakitObat').value,
            rtl: document.getElementById('sakitRtl').value,
            petugas: appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Petugas UKS'
          };

          callBackend('apiSaveSakit', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Data Medis';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalCatatSakit();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              if (res.metrics) {
                appState.metrics = res.metrics;
                renderMetrics(res.metrics);
              }
              appState.cache.sakit = null;
              appState.cache.siswa = null;
              fetchSakitData();
              fetchSiswaData();
              fetchInitialData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Data Medis';
          });
        }

        function tandaiSembuhById(idSakit) {
          const petugas = appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Piket Asrama';
          showToast('Memproses status sembuh siswa...', 'info');

          callBackend('apiUpdateStatusSehat', [idSakit, petugas], function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              showToast(res.message, 'success');

              // 1. Perbarui status catatan sakit di tabel & cache sakit secara live
              if (appState.cache.sakit) {
                const item = appState.cache.sakit.find(function (it) {
                  return String(it.id).trim() === String(idSakit).trim();
                });
                if (item) {
                  item.status = 'Sembuh/Sehat';
                  renderSakitTable(appState.cache.sakit);
                }
              }

              // 2. Perbarui status siswa menjadi Hadir di Asrama di cache siswa & tabel Siswa & QR
              if (appState.cache.siswa) {
                const target = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (s) {
                  const matchNisn = res.nisn && String(s.nisn).trim() === String(res.nisn).trim();
                  const matchNama = res.nama && String(s.nama).trim().toLowerCase() === String(res.nama).trim().toLowerCase();
                  return matchNisn || matchNama;
                });
                if (target) {
                  target.statusAsrama = 'Hadir di Asrama';
                  renderSiswaTable(appState.cache.siswa);
                }
              }

              // 3. Perbarui metrik dan grafik dashboard seketika
              if (res.metrics) {
                appState.metrics = res.metrics;
                renderMetrics(res.metrics);
              }

              // 4. Sinkronisasi data menyeluruh di latar belakang
              fetchSakitData();
              fetchSiswaData();
              fetchInitialData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal memperbarui status sembuh.', 'error');
            }
          });
        }

        // Pelanggaran & Asesmen
        function fetchPelanggaranData() {
          callBackend('apiGetPelanggaranList', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.pelanggaran = res.data;
              renderPelanggaranTable(res.data);
            }
          });
        }

        function renderPelanggaranTable(list) {
          const tbody = document.getElementById('tbodyAsesmen');
          if (!tbody) return;
          tbody.innerHTML = '';

          if (!list || list.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada catatan pelanggaran atau asesmen.</td></tr>';
            return;
          }

          list.forEach(function (item) {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
            tr.innerHTML =
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-500 font-mono text-xs">' + item.tanggalCatat + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-bold text-slate-900">' + item.nama + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 font-medium text-navy">' + (item.noKamar || '-') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-medium text-slate-800">' + item.uraianMasalah + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600">' + item.pihakTerlibat + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-700">' + item.penanganan + '</td>' +
              '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-500 italic">' + item.rtl + '</td>';
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
        }

        function autoFillSiswaAsesmen() {
          const nisn = document.getElementById('asesmenSiswaSelect').value;
          if (!appState.cache.siswa) return;
          const s = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (item) { return item.nisn === nisn; });

          if (s) {
            if (document.getElementById('asesmenAutoNisn')) document.getElementById('asesmenAutoNisn').innerText = s.nisn;
            if (document.getElementById('asesmenAutoJk')) document.getElementById('asesmenAutoJk').innerText = s.jenisKelamin || '-';
            if (document.getElementById('asesmenAutoKamar')) document.getElementById('asesmenAutoKamar').innerText = s.noKamar || '-';
          } else {
            if (document.getElementById('asesmenAutoNisn')) document.getElementById('asesmenAutoNisn').innerText = '-';
            if (document.getElementById('asesmenAutoJk')) document.getElementById('asesmenAutoJk').innerText = '-';
            if (document.getElementById('asesmenAutoKamar')) document.getElementById('asesmenAutoKamar').innerText = '-';
          }
        }

        function openModalCatatAsesmen() {
          document.getElementById('formCatatAsesmen').reset();
          autoFillSiswaAsesmen();
          document.getElementById('modalCatatAsesmen').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalCatatAsesmen() {
          document.getElementById('modalCatatAsesmen').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitCatatAsesmen() {
          const nisn = document.getElementById('asesmenSiswaSelect').value;
          if (!nisn) {
            showToast('Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }
          const s = appState.cache.siswa.find(function (item) { return item.nisn === nisn; });
          if (!s) return;

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveAsesmen');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          const data = {
            nisn: s.nisn,
            nama: s.nama,
            jenisKelamin: s.jenisKelamin,
            noKamar: s.noKamar || '-',
            uraianMasalah: document.getElementById('asesmenUraian').value,
            pihakTerlibat: document.getElementById('asesmenTerlibat').value,
            penanganan: document.getElementById('asesmenPenanganan').value,
            rtl: document.getElementById('asesmenRtl').value,
            petugas: appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Wali Asuh'
          };

          callBackend('apiSavePelanggaran', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Catatan';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalCatatAsesmen();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              appState.cache.pelanggaran = null;
              fetchPelanggaranData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Catatan';
          });
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODUL RENCANA KEBUTUHAN & INVENTARIS
        // =====================================================================

        function switchKebutuhanSubTab(tab) {
          appState.kebutuhanSubTab = tab;
          const tUsulan = document.getElementById('tabUsulanKebutuhan');
          const tInv = document.getElementById('tabInventarisAset');
          const pUsulan = document.getElementById('subtab-kebutuhan-usulan');
          const pInv = document.getElementById('subtab-kebutuhan-inventaris');

          if (tab === 'usulan') {
            tUsulan.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-navy border-b-2 border-navy';
            tInv.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800';
            pUsulan.classList.remove('hidden');
            pInv.classList.add('hidden');
          } else {
            tInv.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-bold text-navy border-b-2 border-navy';
            tUsulan.className = 'pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800';
            pInv.classList.remove('hidden');
            pUsulan.classList.add('hidden');
          }
        }

        function fetchKebutuhanInventarisData() {
          callBackend('apiGetKebutuhanDanInventaris', undefined, function (res) {
            if (res && res.success) {
              appState.cache.kebutuhan = res.kebutuhan;
              appState.cache.inventaris = res.inventaris;
              renderKebutuhanDanInventarisTables();
            }
          });
        }

        function renderKebutuhanDanInventarisTables() {
          // 1. Tabel Kebutuhan
          const tbodyK = document.getElementById('tbodyKebutuhan');
          if (tbodyK) {
            tbodyK.innerHTML = '';
            const listK = appState.cache.kebutuhan;
            if (!listK || listK.length === 0) {
              tbodyK.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada usulan kebutuhan asrama.</td></tr>';
            } else {
              const isAsramaOrAdmin = appState.currentUser && (appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase() === 'admin' || appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase().indexOf('asrama') !== -1);

              listK.forEach(function (item) {
                const isDiterima = (item.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('diterima') !== -1;
                const statusBadge = isDiterima
                  ? '<span class="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-emerald-200">Sudah Diterima</span>'
                  : '<span class="bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] border border-amber-200">Diusulkan</span>';

                let btnTerima = '';
                if (!isDiterima && isAsramaOrAdmin) {
                  btnTerima = '<button onclick="openTerimaKebutuhanModal(\'' + item.id + '\', \'' + item.namaBarang + '\', ' + item.jumlah + ')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm transition">Tandai Diterima & Masuk Aset</button>';
                }

                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
                tr.innerHTML =
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-800">' + item.namaBarang + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-900">' + item.jumlah + ' Unit</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono text-slate-600">Rp ' + Number(item.estimasiHarga || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-500">' + item.tanggal + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600">' + item.pemohon + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3">' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">' + btnTerima + '</td>';
                tbodyK.appendChild(tr);
              });
            }
          }

          // 2. Tabel Inventaris
          const tbodyInv = document.getElementById('tbodyInventaris');
          if (tbodyInv) {
            tbodyInv.innerHTML = '';
            const listInv = appState.cache.inventaris;
            if (!listInv || listInv.length === 0) {
              tbodyInv.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">Belum ada barang inventaris terdaftar.</td></tr>';
            } else {
              const isAsramaOrAdmin = appState.currentUser && (appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase() === 'admin' || appState.currentUser.role.toLowerCase().indexOf('asrama') !== -1);

              listInv.forEach(function (item, idx) {
                const defItemFoto = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1595428774223-ef52624120d2?w=300';
                const foto = formatImageUrl(item.fotoBarang, defItemFoto);
                let editBtn = '';
                if (isAsramaOrAdmin) {
                  editBtn = '<button onclick="openEditInventarisByIndex(' + idx + ')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-navy hover:bg-navy-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-medium">Edit Jumlah/Kondisi</button>';
                }

                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = 'hover:bg-slate-50 transition';
                tr.innerHTML =
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-navy">' + item.noBarang + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-4 flex items-center space-x-3">' +
                  '<img src="' + foto + '" class="w-10 h-10 object-cover rounded border border-slate-200" onerror="this.src=\'' + defItemFoto + '\'">' +
                  '<div><span class="font-bold text-slate-900">' + item.namaBarang + '</span></div>' +
                  '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-2 font-bold text-slate-800">' + item.jumlah + ' Unit</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-emerald-700">' + item.kondisi + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600">' + (item.lokasiKamar || '-') + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-500">' + item.tanggalTerima + '</td>' +
                  '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">' + editBtn + '</td>';
                tbodyInv.appendChild(tr);
              });
            }
          }
        }

        function openModalTambahKebutuhan() {
          document.getElementById('formTambahKebutuhan').reset();
          document.getElementById('modalTambahKebutuhan').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalTambahKebutuhan() {
          document.getElementById('modalTambahKebutuhan').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitTambahKebutuhan() {
          const data = {
            namaBarang: document.getElementById('kebNamaBarang').value,
            jumlah: document.getElementById('kebJumlah').value,
            estimasiHarga: document.getElementById('kebHarga').value,
            pemohon: appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Wali Asrama'
          };

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveKebutuhan');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          callBackend('apiSaveKebutuhan', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Ajukan Usulan';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalTambahKebutuhan();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              appState.cache.kebutuhan = null;
              fetchKebutuhanInventarisData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Ajukan Usulan';
          });
        }

        function openTerimaKebutuhanModal(id, nama, jml) {
          document.getElementById('terimaIdKebutuhan').value = id;
          document.getElementById('terimaNamaBarang').innerText = nama;
          document.getElementById('terimaJumlah').value = jml;
          document.getElementById('modalTerimaKebutuhan').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalTerimaKebutuhan() {
          document.getElementById('modalTerimaKebutuhan').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitTerimaKebutuhan() {
          const fileInput = document.getElementById('terimaFotoFile');
          if (!fileInput || !fileInput.files || !fileInput.files[0]) {
            showToast('Unggah foto fisik barang terlebih dahulu.', 'error');
            return;
          }

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveTerima');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Foto...';

          compressImageFile(fileInput.files[0], 1000, 1000, 0.78, function (fotoBase64) {
            btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';
            const payload = {
              idKebutuhan: document.getElementById('terimaIdKebutuhan').value,
              jumlahDiterima: document.getElementById('terimaJumlah').value,
              kondisi: document.getElementById('terimaKondisi').value,
              lokasiKamar: document.getElementById('terimaLokasi').value,
              fotoBase64: fotoBase64,
              petugas: appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Wali Asrama'
            };

            callBackend('apiTerimaKebutuhanKeInventaris', payload, function (res) {
              btn.disabled = false;
              btn.innerText = 'Terima & Registrasi Aset';
              if (res && res.success) {
                closeModalTerimaKebutuhan();
                showToast(res.message, 'success');
                appState.cache.kebutuhan = null;
                appState.cache.inventaris = null;
                fetchKebutuhanInventarisData();
              } else {
                showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal registrasi barang.', 'error');
              }
            }, function () {
              btn.disabled = false;
              btn.innerText = 'Terima & Registrasi Aset';
            });
          });
        }

        function openEditInventarisByIndex(idx) {
          const item = appState.cache.inventaris[idx];
          if (!item) return;

          document.getElementById('editInvNoBarang').value = item.noBarang;
          document.getElementById('editInvNama').innerText = item.namaBarang;
          document.getElementById('editInvJumlah').value = item.jumlah;
          document.getElementById('editInvKondisi').value = item.kondisi;
          document.getElementById('editInvLokasi').value = item.lokasiKamar || '';
          document.getElementById('modalEditInventaris').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalEditInventaris() {
          document.getElementById('modalEditInventaris').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitEditInventaris() {
          const data = {
            noBarang: document.getElementById('editInvNoBarang').value,
            jumlah: document.getElementById('editInvJumlah').value,
            kondisi: document.getElementById('editInvKondisi').value,
            lokasiKamar: document.getElementById('editInvLokasi').value,
            petugas: appState.currentUser ? appState.currentUser.namaLengkap : 'Wali Asrama'
          };

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveEditInv');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          callBackend('apiUpdateInventaris', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Perubahan';
            if (res && res.success) {
              closeModalEditInventaris();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
              appState.cache.inventaris = null;
              fetchKebutuhanInventarisData();
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan Perubahan';
          });
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // PENGATURAN LOGO & INSTANSI (ADMIN ONLY)
        // =====================================================================

        function previewLogoFile(input) {
          if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
            compressImageFile(input.files[0], 800, 800, 0.85, function (base64) {
              const prev = document.getElementById('previewLogoSettings');
              if (prev) prev.src = base64;
            });
          }
        }

        function openModalSettings() {
          document.getElementById('setNamaInstansi').value = appState.settings.NAMA_INSTANSI || '';
          document.getElementById('setLogoUrl').value = appState.settings.LOGO_URL || '';
          document.getElementById('setAlamat').value = appState.settings.ALAMAT || '';
          document.getElementById('setKontak').value = appState.settings.KONTAK || '';
          const prev = document.getElementById('previewLogoSettings');
          if (prev) prev.src = formatImageUrl(appState.settings.LOGO_URL, 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/3135/3135768.png');
          const fileInput = document.getElementById('setLogoFile');
          if (fileInput) fileInput.value = '';
          document.getElementById('modalSettings').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeModalSettings() {
          document.getElementById('modalSettings').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function submitSettings() {
          const data = {
            NAMA_INSTANSI: document.getElementById('setNamaInstansi').value,
            LOGO_URL: document.getElementById('setLogoUrl').value,
            ALAMAT: document.getElementById('setAlamat').value,
            KONTAK: document.getElementById('setKontak').value
          };

          const btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveSettings');
          btn.disabled = true;
          btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';

          const fileInput = document.getElementById('setLogoFile');
          if (fileInput && fileInput.files && fileInput.files[0]) {
            btn.innerText = 'Mengompres Logo...';
            compressImageFile(fileInput.files[0], 800, 800, 0.85, function (base64) {
              data.logoBase64 = base64;
              btn.innerText = 'Menyimpan...';
              sendSettingsData(data, btn);
            });
          } else {
            sendSettingsData(data, btn);
          }
        }

        function sendSettingsData(data, btn) {
          callBackend('apiUpdateSettings', data, function (res) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan & Sinkronkan';
            if (res && res.success) {
              if (res.logoUrl) {
                data.LOGO_URL = res.logoUrl;
              }
              appState.settings = Object.assign(appState.settings, data);
              syncSettingsToUI();
              closeModalSettings();
              showToast(res.message, 'success');
            } else {
              showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal.', 'error');
            }
          }, function () {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerText = 'Simpan & Sinkronkan';
          });
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // HELPER CETAK & TOAST
        // =====================================================================

        function printElement(elId) {
          const targetEl = document.getElementById(elId);
          if (!targetEl) return;

          const printArea = document.createElement('div');
          printArea.id = 'printArea';

          const clone = targetEl.cloneNode(true);
          if (elId === 'idCardContainer') {
            clone.classList.add('idcard-print-target');
          }
          printArea.appendChild(clone);
          document.body.appendChild(printArea);

          window.print();
          document.body.removeChild(printArea);
        }

        function showToast(message, type) {
          const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
          if (!container) return;

          const toast = document.createElement('div');
          let bg = 'bg-slate-900 text-white';
          if (type === 'success') bg = 'bg-emerald-600 text-white';
          else if (type === 'error') bg = 'bg-red-600 text-white';
          else if (type === 'info') bg = 'bg-navy text-white';

          toast.className = bg + ' px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center space-x-2 transition-all transform duration-300 pointer-events-auto';
          toast.innerHTML = '<span>' + message + '</span>';

          container.appendChild(toast);
          setTimeout(function () {
            toast.style.opacity = '0';
            setTimeout(function () {
              if (toast.parentNode) toast.parentNode.removeChild(toast);
            }, 300);
          }, 3500);
        }
